Retrospective review preserved the two backward-supported activations: protective covering and renewed growth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B007","root_001533/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2a66ce02a8b8934ec808","text":"3. **Womb-like return into guarded life.** Lexical evidence: `ج ن ن B007` fetus hidden in the womb; `ر ح م B003` womb from the basmalah; `ن ف س B005` birth/postpartum bleeding; `ر ج ع B001` return. Structural cue: opening mercy/womb imagery and final covered garden enclose the whole surah. Assigned role: the final garden is a generative hidden place where life is guarded. Abductive move: I infer a birth-return arc from opening `رحم`, final `جنة`, and the addressed `نفس`. Reading change: the ending reads as a return into protected generativity, not mere reward-location.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_455837a7b79c93030852","text":"2. **Entry into renewed, thickening life.** **Evidence/cue:** `ج ن ن B011` is vigorous intertwined growth and `ج ن ن B014` a fresh beginning; 89:28 supplies restored return (`ر ج ع B014`), and 89:29 first incorporates the self among servants. **Mechanism:** restoration opens a new growth-state whose density is both ecological and communal. The packet supplies return, group-entry, then garden-entry; I infer that the second entry expands restored relation into a living environment. **Reading change:** the final garden is a new beginning that grows around an already reintegrated self.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B003","root_000464/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_54e4e86972dcdba33be4","text":"1. **Garden as protective covering after exposure and restraint.** **Evidence/cue:** `ج ن ن B003` is a tree-covered garden, while `ج ن ن B001/B008` supplies concealment and protective cover; `د خ ل B001` supplies actual entry. The backward window begins with unmatched punishment and binding, then releases the tranquil self toward acceptance. **Mechanism:** dense cover serves as shelter from the exposed punitive trajectory. The packet supplies danger, release, and enclosed destination; I infer protection as the garden's functional role. **Reading change:** *jannati* is a shielding habitat, not only a pleasant location.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_aad73c89a9101bb54176","text":"No later ayah follows, but the full retrospective surprise is circular: the last `جنتي` reactivated the basmalah’s womb/kinship mercy and made the surah’s whole movement run from protective naming to protected entry.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b24049c72be47dff7e17","text":"2. **Fertile reversal of corrupted land and hoarded seed.** Lexical evidence: `ج ن ن B011` thick or vigorous growth; `ح ب ب B001` seed-grain; `ح ي ي B002` earth brought alive by rain and growth; `ف س د B001` corruption as loss of balance. Structural cue: the surah moves from corrupted lands, consumed inheritance, and wealth-love to “My garden.” Assigned role: garden-growth is the final reordering of provision into life. Abductive move: I infer ecological reversal: what was hoarded or spoiled becomes sheltered growth under divine possession. Reading change: `جنتي` becomes not only destination but the repaired form of land, provision, and desire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B003","root_000266/B008","root_000464/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ba3d04538e21fe6532e8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:30:1\",\"root_000464\",[\"B001\"]],[\"89:30:2\",\"root_000266\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Cennet, önceki açıkta kalma ve bağlanmanın karşısında ruhu ağaç örtüsüyle saklayan koruyucu bir yaşam alanıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B003","root_000266/B008","root_000464/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ceb63e2d7fa865ff80e2","text":"1. **Garden as protected, tree-covered concealment.** Lexical evidence: `د خ ل B001` entering; `ج ن ن B003` tree-covered garden; `ج ن ن B001` covering and concealment; `ج ن ن B008` protective cover. Structural cue: the final command repeats `ادخلي`, now with `جنتي` after entry among servants. Assigned role: the garden is a covered enclosure that protects rather than imprisons. Abductive move: I infer that concealment is redeemed here: unlike hidden corruption, this is sheltering cover.
